## 1. Purpose

This is the canonical routing ledger for substantive analytical claim transitions. It prevents early or superseded interpretations from remaining in circulation without an authority state.

Allowed transition states:

- **PRESERVE** — current evidence leaves claim intact.
- **STRENGTHEN** — current evidence increases confidence/scope.
- **REVISE** — claim remains partly useful but formulation changes materially.
- **DOWNGRADE** — claim may apply narrowly but should no longer be used as a general account.
- **REJECT** — claim should not be used as current authority.
- **OPEN** — evidence insufficient; keep unresolved.

A transition applies to the claim, not necessarily to the source document containing the earlier formulation.

The ledger begins with the consolidated P01→P05 N25 main-story claim history from `PJSK_N25_MAIN_STORY_SYNTHESIS.md` §16 and currently integrates longitudinal claim authority through EVENT_0051. EVENT_0030, EVENT_0031, EVENT_0037, EVENT_0040, EVENT_0043, and EVENT_0046 were screened I0; EVENT_0032, EVENT_0038, and EVENT_0041 add evidence without governing claim transitions; EVENT_0033, EVENT_0034, EVENT_0035, EVENT_0036, EVENT_0039, EVENT_0042, EVENT_0044, EVENT_0045, EVENT_0047, and EVENT_0051 supply the later transitions/refinements recorded below.

## 2. N25 — Asahina Mafuyu

### CR-N25-MF-001

**Earlier claim:** Mafuyu never had an accessible self or genuine preference.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** Direct autobiographical evidence establishes earlier preferences, pleasure, and self-authored aspiration, including nursing and ordinary leisure desire. The problem is progressive loss of access, not original absence.  
**Boundary:** P04 synthesis correction.  
**Future watch:** later recovery of preference should be measured as re-access/reconstruction, not creation ex nihilo.

### CR-N25-MF-002

**Earlier claim:** Mafuyu is simply indecisive.  
**Transition:** **REJECT as inadequate**.  
**Current authority:** The core problem is impaired access to first-person authority, not ordinary choice difficulty between known preferences.  
**Future watch:** distinguish preference absence/weakness from strategic indecision.

### CR-N25-MF-003

**Earlier claim:** Mafuyu's mother simply commands and controls her through overt coercion.  
**Transition:** **REVISE**.  
**Current authority:** The mother is central, but the mechanism frequently operates through care, rationalization, praise, preselected opportunity, and assumption. EVENT_0035 adds direct evidence that genuine remembered warmth and child attachment can coexist with that later/current autonomy-eroding structure. Strong harm does not require conscious malicious intent, and later harm does not retroactively make every early caring interaction false.  
**Future watch:** later events may clarify intention, escalation, or Mafuyu's own interpretation.

### CR-N25-MF-004

**Earlier claim:** the family alone creates Mafuyu's good-girl identity.  
**Transition:** **DOWNGRADE**.  
**Current authority:** Reinforcement is distributed across family, teachers, peers, achievement structures, and Mafuyu's learned adaptation.  
**Future watch:** separate causal origin from maintenance.

### CR-N25-MF-005

**Earlier claim:** Snow is a consciously fabricated false persona.  
**Transition:** **REVISE / OPEN at mechanism level**.  
**Current authority:** Snow is explicitly differentiated, but the source does not prove that all Snow warmth is strategic fabrication. Behavior may be role-conditioned, automatic, partly genuine, or expressive of material Mafuyu cannot consciously integrate.  
**Future watch:** later spontaneous warmth, role slippage, or explicit self-reflection.

### CR-N25-MF-006

**Earlier claim:** Mafuyu's desire to disappear is not genuine.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** It is genuine within the terminal state.  
**Future watch:** do not treat later recurrence as proof the main-story resolution “failed”; the main story never invalidated the desire as unreal.

### CR-N25-MF-007

**Earlier claim:** Mafuyu's deepest revealed wish is simply to live.  
**Transition:** **DOWNGRADE / OPEN**.  
**Current authority:** The direct main-story discovery is narrower: Mafuyu wanted to be found and is willing to continue searching a little longer.  
**Future watch:** later sources may establish stronger positive life-goals.

### CR-N25-MF-008

**Earlier claim:** Mafuyu is generally passive in relationships.  
**Transition:** **REJECT as general rule**.  
**Current authority:** Endpoint behavior includes chosen proximity, requests for reflection, private initiation, real-name address, gratitude, and direct crisis confrontation. Agency is context-conditioned.  
**Future watch:** ordinary social initiative outside Kanade/Nightcord.

## 3. N25 — Yoisaki Kanade

### CR-N25-K-001

**Earlier claim:** Kanade's desire to help is created entirely by trauma.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** Prosocial artistic value predates the father's collapse; trauma converts aspiration into command.

### CR-N25-K-002

**Earlier claim:** Kanade's salvation project is purely altruistic.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** Genuine care is entangled with guilt, self-worth, ego, and Kanade's need to sustain a reason for creating/living.

### CR-N25-K-003

**Earlier claim:** Kanade caused her father's collapse in the simple way she believes.  
**Transition:** **DOWNGRADE / audience correction required**.  
**Current authority:** Kanade's guilt is psychologically real; source evidence establishes a multicausal professional/psychological crisis that she compresses into personal blame.  
**Epistemic guardrail:** Mafuyu and others should not automatically possess the audience correction.

### CR-N25-K-004

**Earlier claim:** Kanade is passive because she is quiet.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** Task/mission agency is consistently high; she coordinates production, initiates intervention, and persists under uncertainty.

### CR-N25-K-005

**Earlier claim:** Kanade cannot recognize selfish or coercive aspects of rescue.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** She can identify ego, unintended harm, uncertainty, and the possibility that intervention becomes another curse.

### CR-N25-K-006

**Earlier claim:** recognizing rescue pathology gives Kanade healthy boundaries.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** Insight does not dissolve compulsion. Kanade knowingly accepts an indefinite reciprocal promise and remains prone to responsibility inflation.

### CR-N25-K-007

**Earlier claim:** partial efficacy has no value to Kanade.  
**Transition:** **REVISE**.  
**Current authority:** She initially operates in saved/failed terms; by endpoint she begins to value reaching/contact without complete rescue.  
**Future watch:** whether later events stabilize “reaching” as a genuine corrective or whether she reverts to binary rescue logic.

## 4. N25 — Shinonome Ena

### CR-N25-E-001

**Earlier claim:** Ena mainly wants attention.  
**Transition:** **REVISE strongly**.  
**Current authority:** The central need is recognition of the artistic self. Generic appearance-based social attention is an inadequate substitute.

### CR-N25-E-002

**Earlier claim:** the OWN reveal makes Ena genuinely want Mafuyu gone.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** Claimed indifference collapses when Ena confronts the actual loss of Mafuyu and OWN's future work.

### CR-N25-E-003

**Earlier claim:** Ena's concern is straightforward empathy.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** Concern is mixed with envy, resentment, artistic hunger, dignity, and attachment.

### CR-N25-E-004

**Earlier claim:** conflict means weak attachment for Ena.  
**Transition:** **DOWNGRADE**.  
**Current authority:** Main-story evidence explicitly demonstrates care through confrontation; antagonism can be part of attachment rather than its negation.

## 5. N25 — Akiyama Mizuki

### CR-N25-MZ-001

**Earlier claim:** Mizuki's humor is primarily a false mask.  
**Transition:** **DOWNGRADE / preserve caution**.  
**Current authority:** Humor can be protective, but the main-story endpoint strongly supports genuine enjoyment and positive social function.

### CR-N25-MZ-002

**Earlier claim:** Mizuki's problem is the same as Mafuyu's.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** Their autonomy structures are nearly inverse: Mafuyu often lacks accessible preference; Mizuki knows preference and faces external pressure against its self-authorizing status.

### CR-N25-MZ-003

**Earlier claim:** Mizuki understands Mafuyu completely.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** Mizuki operates through strong structural analogy while explicitly preserving Mafuyu's epistemic ownership.

### CR-N25-MZ-004

**Earlier claim:** Mizuki is merely the comic/social member.  
**Transition:** **REJECT as inadequate**.  
**Current authority:** Mizuki provides sophisticated norm-pressure detection, bounded empathy, practical mediation, and social facilitation.

## 6. Cross-character / structural claims

### CR-N25-S-001

**Earlier claim:** N25's four humans share essentially the same pathology.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** They share structurally related threats to first-person authority but differ sharply in mechanism, coping, and developmental problem.

### CR-N25-S-002

**Earlier claim:** Kanade simply saves Mafuyu.  
**Transition:** **REJECT as a governing summary**.  
**Current authority:** The main story distinguishes saving, finding, reaching, and recognition. Mafuyu's endpoint is provisional continuation and desire to be found, not completed rescue.

### CR-N25-S-003

**Earlier claim:** de-anonymization turns Nightcord into a conventionally healthy friend group.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** Nightcord becomes a chosen embodied creative relationship with higher conflict tolerance and real support, while dependency risk, incomplete knowledge, friction, and unresolved individual problems remain.

### CR-N25-S-004

**Earlier claim:** greater disclosure automatically equals healthier or deeper relationship.  
**Transition:** **DOWNGRADE / OPEN as general rule**.  
**Current authority:** N25 develops while preserving partial, delayed, and privately owned knowledge. Connection does not require total transparency.

## 7. EVENT_0002 — 囚われのマリオネット claim transitions

### CR-N25-MF-009

**Earlier claim:** Mafuyu literally cannot feel meaningful affect.  
**Transition:** **REVISE**.  
**Current authority:** Mafuyu shows severe disruption in affect access, labeling, valuation, and integration. EVENT_0002 demonstrates embodied negative/aesthetic/relational affect; EVENT_0035 strengthens this with positive autobiographical warmth that Mafuyu can explicitly say she can feel even while she still cannot name or fully explain it. Do not overcorrect into “normal affect hidden by mistaken belief.”

### CR-N25-MF-010

**Earlier claim:** if another person prompts Mafuyu to express something, the result is externally authored and therefore not genuinely hers.  
**Transition:** **REJECT as a binary rule**.  
**Current authority:** another person can create the occasion for expression while the affect expressed remains Mafuyu's; `PJSK:card:0116` makes the trigger/ownership distinction explicit.

### CR-N25-K-008

**Earlier claim:** Kanade's rescue method is primarily to produce sufficiently powerful music for Mafuyu.  
**Transition:** **REVISE / STRENGTHEN relational component**.  
**Current authority:** Kanade still holds the rescue vow, but now treats Mafuyu's partially expressed interiority as necessary input: observe → ask/listen → transform creatively → return → reassess. Rescue compulsion and self-neglect are PRESERVED.

### CR-N25-E-005

**Earlier claim:** Ena's hostility toward Mafuyu is mainly a rupture signal.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN prior DOWNGRADE**.  
**Current authority:** Mafuyu's judgment matters enough to drive Ena's artistic revision and private concern; confrontation can be a high-investment relationship behavior.

### CR-N25-MZ-005

**Earlier claim:** Mizuki's involvement is largely generalized sociability/social glue.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN rejection of that model**.  
**Current authority:** Mizuki explicitly owns involvement as a personal choice grounded in structural empathy while preserving Mafuyu's epistemic autonomy.

### CR-N25-S-005

**Earlier claim:** the Empty SEKAI/N25 Miku can function as direct access to Mafuyu's feelings because the SEKAI is made from them.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** Miku has privileged contextual/symbolic access but cannot simply read Mafuyu's current mind; uncertainty is source-explicit.

### CR-N25-S-006

**Earlier claim:** N25's post-main conflict tolerance is limited to crisis survival and may not support ordinary unfiltered honesty.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN / REVISE upward**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0002 shows the group absorbing blunt creative criticism, anger, hurt, and teasing without requiring Mafuyu to resume the Snow-style socially optimized presentation.

## 7.2 EVENT_0004 claim transitions

### CR-N25-MF-006 — positive affect can precede explicit appraisal

**Earlier claim:** Mafuyu's affect is impaired in access/labeling rather than globally absent, with EVENT_0002 evidence strongest for negative, aesthetic, and relief-like states.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0004 supplies positive embodied/social evidence: coordinated competition can produce a spontaneous-looking smile before Mafuyu consciously labels the experience.

### CR-N25-MF-007 — public-mask opacity

**Earlier claim:** Mafuyu's public “good girl” performance is highly convincing.  
**Transition:** **REVISE**.  
**Current authority:** the mask remains socially highly effective but is not affectively opaque to every observer. Detectability depends on observer sensitivity and context; ordinary peers accept the presentation while Emu experiences incongruence.

### CR-N25-MF-008 — salience before meaning

**Earlier claim:** self-relevant affect may precede complete verbal explanation.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN**.  
**Current authority:** self-relevant significance may also appear as memory, novelty, voluntary return-to-topic, or behavioral attention before Mafuyu possesses a conscious preference label.

### CR-CROSS-EMU-MF-001 — detection does not equal explanatory authority

**Earlier claim:** none; new guardrail created by EVENT_0004.  
**Transition:** **OPEN → canonical bounded claim**.  
**Current authority:** Emu can detect expression–affect mismatch without knowing its cause. Perceptual sensitivity must not be converted into omniscient authority over Mafuyu's “true feelings.”

## 7.3 EVENT_0007 claim transitions

### CR-N25-MZ-006 — school avoidance is threat-conditioned, not generalized social disinterest

**Earlier claim:** Mizuki's irregular school engagement is strongly associated with scrutiny, normalization pressure, and selective withdrawal.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN + REVISE for precision**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0007 shows that when identity threat is lowered without demanding self-erasure, Mizuki can enter school collective life enthusiastically. Avoidance is substantially self-protective/context-conditioned rather than evidence of low social appetite.

### CR-N25-MZ-007 — belonging versus conformity

**Earlier claim:** Mizuki treats chosen aesthetic preference as self-authorizing and resists pressure to become “normal.”  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN + REVISE**.  
**Current authority:** Mizuki rejects coerced sameness but can strongly desire belonging. The class T-shirt sequence shows pain at exclusion from ordinary matching and eventual acceptance of the group marker through self-authored customization.

### CR-N25-MZ-008 — humor/playfulness is genuine baseline

**Earlier claim:** Mizuki's humor should not be flattened into a defensive mask.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0007 supplies extensive low-stakes enjoyment, aesthetic excitement, teasing, spontaneous laughter, and rapid social bonding in contexts where defense is not the primary function.

### CR-N25-MZ-009 — partial disclosure can coexist with real trust

**Earlier claim:** Mizuki uses selective disclosure to preserve autonomy and safety.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN / REVISE**.  
**Current authority:** card `0139:01` explicitly establishes that Mizuki feels safe with An while knowing An has not been told everything. Trust is not binary on complete disclosure.

### CR-N25-MZ-010 — N25 as companion-like ordinary-life relationship

**Earlier claim:** N25 is a chosen creative/social relationship whose exact friendship category remains partly unarticulated for Mizuki.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN**.  
**Current authority:** Mizuki says `仲間` does not quite fit but acknowledges meeting people of that kind, repeatedly imagines N25 in ordinary festival situations, and proposes future group attendance.

### CR-N25-MZ-011 — belonging without assimilation

**Earlier claim:** implicit in the first-person-authority framework but not yet an explicit Mizuki behavioral rule.  
**Transition:** **OPEN → canonical strong claim**.  
**Current authority:** Mizuki can accept collective belonging when self-authorship over presentation is preserved; customizing the class shirt is the clearest direct example.

### CR-N25-MZ-012 — increased participation does not resolve the deeper private issue

**Earlier potential overread:** successful festival participation implies broad school reintegration or resolution of Mizuki's undisclosed identity conflict.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** stigma memories, attendance instability, selective disclosure, and norm-pressure vulnerability remain active after the festival. EVENT_0007 expands response possibilities without removing the underlying risk structure.

### CR-CROSS-FIRSTPERSON-001 — first-person authority recurs beyond N25

**Earlier status:** N25-seeded theme awaiting cross-unit validation.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN / cross-unit validation**.  
**Current authority:** Toya's account of Tsukasa telling him that it is his life, he may do what he wants, and he may say that something he hates is hateful independently validates the first-person-authority structure without implying identical character histories.

## 7.4 EVENT_0009 claim transitions

### CR-N25-G-013 — N25 ordinary-life companionship is enacted, not merely projected

**Earlier claim:** EVENT_0007 established companion-like ordinary-life projection and future social intent.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN SUBSTANTIALLY**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0009 shows all four voluntarily gathering for a New Year's countdown without work/crisis necessity, materially contributing to the occasion, and continuing ordinary social contact afterward.

### CR-N25-SEKAI-014 — Empty SEKAI is not only a crisis/search site

**Earlier potential formulation:** Empty SEKAI functions primarily as Mafuyu's psychological manifestation, crisis arena, and affect-search environment.  
**Transition:** **REVISE**.  
**Current authority:** those functions remain, but EVENT_0009 establishes a durable additional function as chosen social venue and seasonal-relational container.

### CR-N25-MZ-015 — Mizuki adapts activity format around others' constraints

**Earlier claim:** Mizuki seeks ordinary companionship and can facilitate social inclusion.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN / operationalize**.  
**Current authority:** after Ena/Kanade reject an outdoor plan and Mafuyu has schedule constraints, Mizuki redesigns the gathering rather than coercing participation or abandoning the social goal.

### CR-N25-ENA-016 — Ena is not generally leisure-avoidant or antisocial

**Earlier potential overread:** Ena's refusal of Mizuki's outing reflects broad reluctance toward ordinary social leisure.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** the refusal is context-sensitive (cold/outdoor inconvenience). Ena independently initiates an indoor first-sale shopping outing with Mizuki and invests heavily in coordinating it.

### CR-N25-EM-017 — Ena's antagonism toward Mafuyu does not exclude mundane care

**Earlier claim:** Ena-Mafuyu attachment is high-friction but durable.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN**.  
**Current authority:** Ena notices Mafuyu's poor mood, proposes clothes as a diversion, and remains aesthetically attentive despite ongoing irritation and unresolved conflict.

### CR-N25-MIKU-018 — N25 Miku is not only a crisis mediator

**Earlier model:** N25 Miku primarily functions as mirror/mediator within Mafuyu's search.  
**Transition:** **REVISE**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0009 establishes additional manifestation-specific roles as celebration participant, stable waiting presence, and recipient of ordinary care/comfort objects. The non-omniscience guardrail remains unchanged.

## 7.5 EVENT_0014 claim transitions

### CR-N25-ENA-019 - Ena's recognition drive is artistic-legitimacy sensitivity, not simple vanity

**Earlier potential formulation:** Ena wants likes/attention and is therefore externally validation-dependent in a generic social sense.  
**Transition:** **REVISE**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0014 distinguishes ordinary attention from recognition of intended artistic choices. Ena's deepest vulnerability concerns whether the valued creative self is seen as artistically real, especially under the shadow of paternal talent judgment and professional aspiration.

### CR-N25-ENA-020 - genuine love of drawing and desire for recognition coexist

**Earlier claim:** Ena genuinely likes drawing but is highly recognition-sensitive.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN + REVISE for integration**.  
**Current authority:** the event rejects the false choice between intrinsic and extrinsic motive. Ena's love of drawing predates the paternal rupture, while recognition becomes increasingly necessary to sustain the desired artistic identity under perceived talent deficiency.

### CR-N25-ENA-021 - uncertainty about talent no longer has automatic authority to end creation

**Earlier state:** Ena's shame/anger/effort cycle could collapse into disappearance or domain abandonment when artistic legitimacy failed.  
**Transition:** **REVISE / developmental**.  
**Current authority:** card `0175` explicitly shows Ena interrupting the "genius / no talent" comparison with a new rule: she does not want to give up, so she continues. This supports `E-E0014-01`.

### CR-N25-ENA-022 - needing recognition is not treated as evidence of fraudulent artistic motivation

**Earlier crisis belief:** if Ena cannot draw without being recognized, then her reason to draw is defective and she should hate that part of herself.  
**Transition:** **REJECT as current authority**.  
**Current authority:** Mafuyu's intervention directly de-pathologizes recognition desire. Ena can want recognition, fear its absence, and still choose continued creation.

### CR-N25-FAMILY-023 - Ena's father is harshly protective/professional, not simply malicious

**Earlier potential overread:** the father's discouragement is primarily ridicule, cruelty, or desire to suppress Ena.  
**Transition:** **REVISE**.  
**Current authority:** his private conversation with Akito frames the warning as professional knowledge of isolation, brutal competition, and suffering. The event simultaneously preserves the damage caused by global talent disqualification and the failure to respond as a parent.

### CR-N25-FAMILY-024 - good intent does not equal relational repair

**Earlier possible correction:** once the father's concern is known, Ena should reinterpret the history benignly.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** Ena hears from Akito that the father was worried and still cannot forgive him. Repair begins only when the father actually looks at a specific work and gives specific recognition; even then the relationship remains injured.

### CR-N25-EM-025 - Ena-Mafuyu conflict becomes a deliberately used creative resource

**Earlier claim:** the dyad supports productive confrontation despite envy/irritation.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN SUBSTANTIALLY / REVISE**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0014 shows Ena actively requesting Mafuyu's criticism, accepting useful content despite harsh phrasing, receiving rare positive recognition, and converting talent envy into a competitive goal of making work Mafuyu will genuinely like.

### CR-N25-MF-026 - "none" and "unknown" are analytically distinct for Mafuyu

**Earlier claim:** Mafuyu lacks reliable access to ordinary preference, but absence versus inaccessibility remains unresolved.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN as an available distinction; keep underlying fact OPEN**.  
**Current authority:** Ena explicitly tells Mafuyu that having nothing and not knowing are different. Mafuyu considers the proposition. This does not prove a hidden stable preference set.

### CR-N25-K-027 - Kanade can use bounded reaching/support outside the Mafuyu salvation schema

**Earlier claim:** EVENT_0002 revised Kanade toward relational affect retrieval while preserving rescue compulsion.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN / broaden domain**.  
**Current authority:** with Ena, Kanade acknowledges incomplete causal knowledge, creates a message that communicates companionship rather than cure, supplies concrete evidence of Ena's artistic value, and can then trust/wait rather than escalating intervention.

### CR-N25-MZ-028 - care can require waiting rather than continued intervention

**Earlier claim:** Mizuki is a socially active, autonomy-sensitive support/facilitation figure.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN / operationalize restraint**.  
**Current authority:** card `0179` shows Mizuki wanting to keep doing more for Ena, then accepting that after Ena has reclaimed agency, trusting and waiting is the more appropriate expression of care.

### CR-N25-G-029 - Nightcord vulnerability/support is reciprocal, not Mafuyu-only

**Earlier group model:** Nightcord can survive conflict, support Mafuyu, and enact ordinary companionship.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN / REVISE upward**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0014 makes Ena the endangered member and Mafuyu one of the consequential interveners. The group can mobilize creative care for different members without requiring identical support roles.

### CR-N25-SEKAI-030 - Empty SEKAI is dynamically shared after relational acceptance

**Earlier claim:** EVENT_0009 added ordinary social use to Mafuyu's crisis/search space.  
**Transition:** **REVISE**.  
**Current authority:** N25 Miku explicitly links Rin's manifestation to the SEKAI changing after Mafuyu accepted Kanade, Ena, and Mizuki. Mafuyu remains foundational, but other members' wishes may participate in manifestation history.

### CR-N25-RIN-031 - continued creation is both potentially curse-like and potentially life-sustaining

**Earlier thematic risk:** "keep creating" is either an uncomplicated good or only a pathological obligation.  
**Transition:** **REJECT binary / PRESERVE dialectic**.  
**Current authority:** N25 Rin explicitly recognizes the phrase as curse-like from one angle while also seeing it as a form of rescue and relational bond for Ena at this state boundary.

## 7.6 EVENT_0019 claim transitions

### CR-N25-MZ-032 - Mizuki has a known/chosen self rather than a Mafuyu-like missing self

**Earlier risk:** structural similarity between Mizuki and Mafuyu could be overextended into a shared self-access pathology.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN / REJECT equivalence**.  
**Current authority:** Rin explicitly points to Mizuki's stable expressed preferences - clothes, ribbons, `カワイイ` aesthetics - while MEIKO remains uncertain. Mizuki's problem is social viability/disclosure of known self, not general inability to locate first-person preference.

### CR-N25-MZ-033 - belonging is no longer the only bottleneck

**Earlier claim:** Mizuki's central post-main problem is whether belonging without assimilation is possible.  
**Transition:** **REVISE**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0007-0014 establish enough successful belonging that EVENT_0019 creates a second-order problem: future attachment raises pressure to disclose a guarded self-bound issue that Mizuki expects others will not understand.

### CR-N25-MZ-034 - partial disclosure can coexist with trust, but Mizuki experiences a ceiling

**Earlier claim:** EVENT_0007 supports real trust under incomplete disclosure.  
**Transition:** **PRESERVE + REVISE at EVENT_0019; STRENGTHEN/REVISE through EVENT_0039**.  
**Current authority:** An/Rui/N25 relationships remain genuine despite incomplete disclosure. EVENT_0019 establishes Mizuki's belief that the guarded issue creates a ceiling on deeper `本当の友達`; EVENT_0039 complicates that ceiling by giving explicit friendship naming and durable waiting *before* disclosure. The ceiling remains psychologically real to Mizuki, but it is not an objective rule that undisclosed relationships cannot deepen.

### CR-N25-MZ-035 - cheerful Mizuki is not a sufficient distress indicator

**Earlier reconstruction shortcut:** persistent playfulness implies the relevant distress has passed.  
**Transition:** **REJECT as general rule; STRENGTHEN strongly through EVENT_0039**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0019 and later ordinary-life evidence show persistent disclosure distress coexisting with genuine cheerfulness. EVENT_0039 supplies the strongest case: Mizuki intentionally re-enters a high-energy familiar register while acutely distressed, avoids Nightcord partly because Ena may see through it, and still genuinely enjoys shopping/banter afterward. Brightness can be real affect, social skill, and defense simultaneously.

### CR-N25-G-036 - ordinary companionship can deepen while transparency remains incomplete

**Earlier group model:** EVENT_0009-0014 establish enacted companionship and reciprocal care.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN / REVISE upward**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0019 adds full-day real-world travel, later ordinary outings, and explicit future-oriented attachment while leaving Mizuki's central guarded issue unknown to the group. Closeness and epistemic asymmetry are not opposites.

### CR-N25-ENA-037 - Ena can support under unexplained interpersonal concern

**Earlier model:** Ena is direct, conflict-capable, and increasingly able to use ordinary care.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN**.  
**Current authority:** Ena notices Mizuki's concealed distress, asks without obtaining an explanation, checks limited context, then offers shopping companionship rather than forcing disclosure.

### CR-N25-K-038 - Kanade can seek help when solitary compositional control fails

**Earlier model:** Kanade copes with threat by increased task effort and mission persistence.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN / broaden coping repertoire**.  
**Current authority:** during EVENT_0019's slump Kanade explicitly seeks group consultation and accepts unfamiliar experience as a route back into composition. The salvation mission remains intact.

### CR-N25-MF-039 - tentative positive affect can support future ordinary choice

**Earlier claim:** Mafuyu can report uncertain/negative affect as legitimate self-data after EVENT_0002.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0019 shows `おもしろかったような気がする` developing into a private willingness to repeat a similar trip. Preference can emerge through tentative salience without strong certainty language.

### CR-N25-VS-040 - N25 MEIKO is not an authoritative decoder by default

**Earlier risk:** a newly manifested Virtual Singer may be treated as possessing the correct explanation of the human character who drew their attention.  
**Transition:** **REJECT / constrain**.  
**Current authority:** MEIKO explicitly lacks her own purpose and Mizuki's answer, withholds conclusions due insufficient information, and receives correction from Rin. Observation is a method, not omniscience.

### CR-N25-MZ-041 - future attachment increases both disclosure desire and withdrawal/deferral pressure

**Earlier status:** EVENT_0019 left directionality `OPEN`.  
**Transition:** **REVISE / resolve directionality through EVENT_0039**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0039 demonstrates simultaneous opposing pressure. Mizuki wants to answer Ena's explicit friendship and waiting with honest disclosure, while the same attachment makes preserving the current relationship so valuable that nondisclosure becomes a conscious deferral strategy. Greater attachment can therefore increase both approach and avoidance rather than selecting one monotonic direction.


## 7.7 EVENT_0026 claim transitions

### CR-N25-K-042 - Kanade can value intermediate efficacy without calling it complete salvation

**Earlier claim:** Kanade's outcome absolutism strongly discounts partial reach when Mafuyu is not fully “saved.”  
**Transition:** **REVISE / DOWNGRADE at EVENT_0026; STRENGTHEN through EVENT_0036**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0026 card `0237` explicitly allows both propositions at once: Kanade has not reached “saving,” and Mafuyu's smile still represents real forward movement. EVENT_0034 card `0281:02` then cross-validates that correction outside the original event: Kanade reports that a recent song reached the person she wants to help “a little” and explicitly says that experience taught her to think about how she wants listeners to feel. Intermediate efficacy is therefore becoming a reusable creative method rather than a one-off consolation. EVENT_0035 then supplies the strongest realized case so far: the same positive-destination song becomes an affect-memory cue through which Mafuyu independently recovers and preserves warmth without being fully “saved.” This strengthens `K-E0026-01` without removing its persistent-penance half.

### CR-N25-K-043 - Kanade's Mafuyu goal is no longer adequately described as non-disappearance

**Earlier claim:** preventing Mafuyu's disappearance is the dominant rescue endpoint.  
**Transition:** **REVISE**.  
**Current authority:** Kanade explicitly identifies non-disappearance as partly her own ego and then specifies a positive desired lived state: Mafuyu finding herself, feeling more broadly, experiencing warmth, and genuinely smiling.

### CR-N25-K-044 - Kanade's father is positive musical inheritance as well as guilt object

**Earlier overcompression:** father-related musical material functions primarily as trauma/guilt trigger.  
**Transition:** **REVISE / STRENGTHEN complexity**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0026 recovers music-box timbre, gentle/enveloping warmth, ordinary family happiness, and the father's criterion of making loved people smile. The guilt function remains active.

### CR-N25-K-045 - Kanade has not accepted a multicausal correction to her father's collapse

**Potential overread:** recovering happy memory and a positive destination means Kanade has relinquished self-blame.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** Kanade still says she has no right to grieve and ties her right to remain to whether she can save Mafuyu. Positive memory becomes usable without acquittal.

### CR-N25-K-046 - EVENT_0026 is not a healthy-self-care transition

**Potential overread:** broader perspective and ordinary support imply improved bodily boundaries.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** card `0237` directly confirms long periods without food/water and Kanade's normalization that she is used to this.

### CR-N25-MF-047 - spontaneous positive affect can precede Mafuyu's awareness

**Earlier risk:** positive affect is absent unless Mafuyu can consciously label it.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** Mafuyu smiles before noticing that she is smiling and then weakly identifies warmth.

### CR-N25-MF-048 - Mafuyu's taste experience is not completely undifferentiated

**Earlier claim:** ordinary taste flattening is severe and broad.  
**Transition:** **PRESERVE severity + REVISE totality**.  
**Current authority:** Mafuyu still cannot identify the caramel-chocolate taste but can judge it less unpleasant than other food. Comparative valence is available before normal taste recognition.

### CR-N25-MF-049 - perceived change can itself become Mafuyu self-search evidence

**Earlier claim:** Mafuyu requires externally returned creative evidence to make internal change legible.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN / broaden**.  
**Current authority:** after Miku explains that Mafuyu/SEKAI have changed, Mafuyu asks what changed and voluntarily tests food partly because the possibility of change has become salient.

### CR-N25-MZ-050 - EVENT_0019 disclosure conflict remains current through EVENT_0026

**Potential overread:** effective support of Kanade or ordinary cheerfulness means the future-attachment conflict has passed.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** card `0240` has Mizuki explicitly fear that recent N25 memories are already becoming past and wonder whether they will be alone again.

### CR-N25-MZ-051 - Mizuki can explicitly recognize N25 members as important while remaining nondisclosing

**Earlier claim:** Mizuki's attachment is often shown indirectly or through difficult friendship labels.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN**.  
**Current authority:** Mizuki privately names Ena, Kanade, and Mafuyu as important and explains continued intervention through that attachment, without resolving the guarded issue.

### CR-N25-ENA-052 - Ena distinguishes inability to articulate from deliberate withholding

**Earlier model:** Ena is broadly impatient with unclear emotional communication.  
**Transition:** **REVISE / sharpen**.  
**Current authority:** card `0241` explicitly contrasts Mafuyu, who often cannot identify/word the state, with Mizuki, whom Ena believes could say more but intentionally does not. Ena still lacks Mizuki's content.

### CR-N25-EM-053 - Ena-Mafuyu conflict is trusted enough to include meta-honesty about the conflict itself

**Earlier claim:** the dyad uses productive antagonistic creative feedback.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN**.  
**Current authority:** Ena admits her push contains displaced envy; Mafuyu thanks Ena for saying difficult things and becoming angry; Ena authorizes continued blunt first-person speech despite irritation.

### CR-N25-VS-054 - N25 Luka's directness does not equal omniscience

**Earlier risk:** Luka's confident questioning may be read as authoritative knowledge of Mafuyu.  
**Transition:** **REJECT / constrain**.  
**Current authority:** Luka explicitly concludes that what she learned is that she does not know, and adopts repeated questioning rather than a fixed diagnosis.
## 7.8 EVENT_0029 claim transitions

### CR-N25-MZ-055 - genuine enjoyment does not falsify the disclosure conflict

**Potential overread:** if Mizuki genuinely enjoys N25 outings, EVENT_0019's attachment/disclosure conflict must have weakened substantially or been performative.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** card `0258` explicitly gives both propositions: Mizuki remembers that the festival was genuinely enjoyable in the moment and afterward experiences a chest-hole-like absence. Future-oriented group language in the core also reactivates hesitation. Positive social affect and guarded-future anxiety can coexist.

### CR-N25-ENA-056 - Ena support can be persistent without becoming disclosure extraction

**Earlier claim:** Ena notices Mizuki's concealed distress and uses low-pressure ordinary companionship when causal access is absent.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN through EVENT_0029; REVISE/STRENGTHEN upward through EVENT_0039**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0039 first shows Ena crossing into pressure and causing rupture, then consciously changing method after MEIKO's caution. Ena explicitly tells Mizuki that speaking is not owed, promises to wait, and resumes ordinary friendship without converting support into payment for information. Persistence and non-extraction are now an explicit relationship rule rather than only an inferred pattern.

### CR-N25-MZ-057 - reliance on Ena becomes more imaginable without making Mizuki disclosure-ready

**Potential overread:** converging advice from Airi and Akito that Mizuki can rely on Ena produces a decisive disclosure transition.  
**Transition:** **REJECT / preserve OPEN conflict**.  
**Current authority:** Mizuki recognizes Ena's caregiving reliability and receives another ordinary invitation, but the guarded issue remains undisclosed. EVENT_0029 increases evidence for relational safety, not proof of disclosure readiness.

### CR-N25-G-058 - N25 ordinary companionship is robust across public social environments

**Earlier claim:** N25 has enacted ordinary companionship and future-oriented shared life outside crisis/production.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0029 adds a crowded public festival, games, live music, food, photos, a souvenir explicitly tied to the group, flexible splitting/recombining, and later memory-sharing with Miku. No new group state is required.

### CR-N25-MF-059 - Mafuyu's polished public mode remains highly competent after N25 integration

**Potential overread:** greater candor with N25 should erode or replace the public good-student presentation.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** card `0255` shows immediate polished first-meeting behavior with Akito. Ena and Mizuki recognize the switch because they now know a lower-burden candid mode, but performance competence remains intact.

### CR-N25-FAMILY-060 - Shinonome sibling antagonism is compatible with reciprocal practical care and causal influence

**Earlier claim:** Ena/Akito are abrasive siblings whose care is often indirect and practical.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN / broaden**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0029 mirrors reciprocal shoe-help across two summer festivals and reveals Ena's casual suggestion to try music as part of the causal path to Akito's chosen musical life; Akito privately credits her with helping lead him to his present companions.

## 7.9 EVENT_0033 claim transitions

EVENT_0032 adds ordinary D0 evidence but does not independently require a governing claim transition. EVENT_0033 does.

### CR-N25-MF-061 - public social competence is compatible with low private investment

**Potential overread:** if Mafuyu's public mode is role-optimized, advice and prosocial behavior produced within it should be treated as merely fake or analytically discounted.  
**Transition:** **REJECT / STRENGTHEN distinction**.  
**Current authority:** core `0033:01` shows Mafuyu noticing Shizuku's concern, accurately limiting her own idol expertise, offering useful seasonal ideas, and causally helping generate the moon-viewing plan. Card `0278:01` then shows that moon-viewing itself is privately low-salience for Mafuyu. EVENT_0036 strengthens the distinction with same-release paired evidence: Mafuyu publicly praises Minori's stage and says she will watch the videos, then privately tells N25 that the performance produced no felt response.

**Current formulation:** public-role behavior can be affectively overdetermined by social expectation while still containing genuine cognition, observation, and practical prosocial competence. `public = fake` is not an authorized model.

### CR-N25-MF-062 - weak non-null valence can precede categorical preference

**Earlier claim:** Mafuyu may register affect/salience before she can identify or label a stable preference.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN**.  
**Current authority:** card `0278:02` moves from Mafuyu's explicit prediction that she will probably feel nothing to `見てても、退屈じゃない` and private `嫌な気分じゃない`, without any claim that she likes moon-viewing.

**Current formulation:** `predicted null -> lived non-null valence -> possible salience -> categorical preference remains OPEN`.

### CR-N25-MF-063 - relational framing can support affect access without authoring the feeling

**Potential overread:** because Kanade's description and Mizuki's invitation precede Mafuyu's weak positive response, the others have defined Mafuyu's feeling for her.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0033 shows Mizuki creating the activity and Kanade describing her own perception while Mafuyu independently evaluates the result. EVENT_0035 strongly extends the same rule: Kanade supplies music, care, and the first-person memories behind her composition; Miku supplies a performance/feedback surface; Mafuyu independently links the affect to her own memory, rejects fuller interpretation when she cannot support it, and preserves it in her own words. External framing can alter access conditions without replacing first-person authority.

## 7.10 EVENT_0034 claim transition

EVENT_0034 does not require a new claim ID. It **STRENGTHENS `CR-N25-K-042`** by demonstrating persistence and generalization of the EVENT_0026 correction.

**Current formulation:** Kanade can recognize partial reach as real evidence, distinguish it from completed salvation, and use recipient-oriented desired effect as a compositional criterion. She can also explicitly recognize that non-solving relational support—believing in and watching over someone—has value, even though her own guilt-driven rescue obligation remains much more demanding than that principle would require.

**Guardrail:** do not overread this as recovery from rescue compulsion, healthy boundaries, or abandonment of self-punitive obligation. EVENT_0034 strengthens the corrective component inside `K-E0026-01`; it does not supersede the state.

## 7.11 EVENT_0035 claim transitions

### `CR-N25-MF-003` - maternal care and autonomy harm are not mutually exclusive

**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN / REVISE for temporal complexity**.

EVENT_0035 directly shows young Mafuyu making self-originated requests for rabbit-shaped apple, maternal presence, and hand-holding, and receiving genuine responsive care. The current authority must therefore preserve both propositions: remembered love/care can be real, and the later/current `good child` expectation system can still erode first-person authority. Reject both monocausal extremes.

### `CR-N25-MF-009` - positive affect can be felt before it is fully named

**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN**.

EVENT_0035 moves the affect-access model beyond weak valence. Mafuyu links present warmth to autobiographical care memory and explicitly recognizes that she can feel it, while remaining unable to say exactly what the feeling is.

### CR-N25-MF-064 - affective access and categorical naming are separable operations

**Potential overread:** if Mafuyu cannot name or explain a feeling, she has not meaningfully accessed it.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0035 provides a stronger sequence than the weak-valence evidence of EVENT_0033: `somatic warmth -> recurrent salience -> autobiographical association -> can feel -> still cannot fully name/explain`. Access, ownership, labeling, and causal interpretation must be modeled as distinct stages.

### CR-N25-MF-065 - self-authored preservation is developmental evidence without completed self-knowledge

**Potential overread:** Mafuyu must first know what a feeling means before a lyric can count as first-person authorship.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** Mafuyu explicitly decides the feeling should be preserved **for herself**, tries to render it **in her own words**, iteratively revises the lyric against what she wants to communicate, and later says she wrote what she wanted to write. EVENT_0036 then demonstrates persistence beyond the writing act: Mafuyu says that if the completed collaborative song exists, she thinks she will not forget the feeling she recovered. Self-authored preservation can therefore be sustained by a shared creative artifact without transferring ownership of the feeling. This remains compatible with incomplete categorical interpretation.

### `CR-N25-MF-063` - relational support can increase access without taking authorship

**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN**.

EVENT_0035 provides the strongest example so far. Kanade's composition and caregiving, Luka's questions, Mizuki's pacing, and Miku's performance all alter the conditions under which Mafuyu can notice and retain affect. Mafuyu nevertheless owns the final evidentiary step: she makes the autobiographical association, withholds claims she cannot support, and chooses the words herself.

### `CR-N25-K-042` - intermediate efficacy becomes observable downstream change

**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN through EVENT_0035**.

The partial reach Kanade identified in EVENT_0026 and generalized as method in EVENT_0034 now produces a concrete downstream change in Mafuyu's search process. Kanade may count this as meaningful evidence without treating it as completed salvation. Persistent rescue/penance remains current.

## 7.12 EVENT_0036 claim transitions

EVENT_0036 requires no new governing claim ID. It strengthens three existing claims through unusually clean cross-context evidence.

### `CR-N25-MF-061` - public competence does not prove private affective investment

**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN through EVENT_0036**.

Mafuyu's polished praise to Minori and stated intention to watch the videos coexist with her later direct private report that she felt nothing from the performance. The correct model is mode-sensitive: public speech can be sincere as socially competent/prosocial action without being a transparent report of private affect.

### `CR-N25-MF-065` - self-authored preservation can persist through collaborative artifact

**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN through EVENT_0036**.

Mafuyu's EVENT_0035 lyric authorship is no longer only a one-scene act. In card `0294:02`, she treats the resulting song as a durable support for remembering the recovered feeling. Collaboration does not erase first-person ownership when the artifact is built around material Mafuyu chose to preserve.

### `CR-N25-K-042` - recipient-oriented method tolerates divergent response

**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN through EVENT_0036**.

Kanade personally receives energy from Minori/idol performance while Mafuyu receives none. Kanade treats her own reaction as creative input but does not overwrite Mafuyu's report or force the bright style into the Mafuyu-directed rescue path. This further stabilizes intermediate-efficacy and recipient-oriented composition inside `K-E0026-01` while persistent rescue/penance remains current.

**Guardrail:** EVENT_0036 does not establish healthy Kanade boundaries, broad Mafuyu preference recovery, or a rule that all public Mafuyu praise is affectively empty.

## 7.13 EVENT_0039 claim transitions

### CR-N25-MZ-066 - anticipated kindness does not equal disclosure safety

**Earlier shortcut:** if Mizuki expects N25 to be accepting or kind, the guarded disclosure should become substantially safe.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0039 explicitly separates expected benevolence from expected relational continuity. Mizuki fears that even well-intentioned kindness after disclosure may change how every interaction is interpreted or handled. Acceptance can therefore remain psychologically threatening without being rejection.

### CR-N25-MZ-067 - nondisclosure can become an attachment-preservation strategy

**Earlier model:** nondisclosure functions primarily as privacy/boundary protection and avoidance of expected invalidation.  
**Transition:** **REVISE / broaden**.  
**Current authority:** after Ena promises to wait, Mizuki privately recognizes a new motive: continuing not to tell may let the current relationship last longer. Silence is now partly strategic temporal preservation of valued ordinary friendship, even while it creates guilt/deception pressure.

**Guardrail:** this does not establish a permanent decision never to disclose.

### CR-N25-VS-068 - MEIKO noninterference is selective rather than absolute passivity

**Earlier claim:** N25 MEIKO's governing method is distant observation/non-directive monitoring.  
**Transition:** **PRESERVE + REVISE**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0039 confirms that MEIKO intends to keep watching from afar, but distance can include confidentiality, low-demand refuge, listening, and limited advice when she judges intervention warranted. Her posture is selective noninterference, not refusal ever to act.

### CR-N25-MF-065 - self-authored preservation can become self-addressed creative authorship

**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN through EVENT_0039**.

Card `0311:01` has Mafuyu distinguish lyrics written to preserve her own recovered feeling from Kanade's other-directed rescue writing. Kanade identifies Mafuyu herself as a real addressee. Mafuyu tentatively accepts continuing to write from her own feelings/thoughts. This strengthens first-person ownership without requiring an external recipient.

### CR-N25-K-042 - intermediate efficacy generalizes to broader listeners

**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN through EVENT_0039**.

Card `0309:01` gives Kanade direct public evidence that the current song leaves meaningful effects in listeners beyond Mafuyu. Kanade cautiously interprets this as reaching them rather than as total salvation. The possible father smile remains uncertain and must not be treated as confirmed efficacy.

### No guarded-content revelation claim

EVENT_0039 does **not** authorize any claim about the exact guarded content beyond what was already source-bounded in the corpus. The event analyzes the social/relational mechanics of concealment and disclosure without requiring the analytical layer to import later-explicated specifics backward.

## 7.14 EVENT_0042 claim transitions

### CR-N25-MF-069 - positive affect can be generated by another person's happiness before categorical relational labeling

**Earlier limitation:** the strongest positive-affect model tied Mafuyu's warmth to music-mediated/autobiographical self-retrieval.  
**Transition:** **REVISE / broaden**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0042 repeatedly shows Mafuyu's chest becoming warm because Kanade is happy and because Mafuyu recognizes that her own small action helped produce that happiness. No immediate song or childhood-memory trigger is required, and Mafuyu still cannot categorically name the relational feeling.

**Current claim:** relationally generated positive affect is valid first-person evidence before friendship/love/preference labels become available.

### CR-N25-MF-070 - beneficial contribution can become self-evidence without becoming a generalized helper identity

**Earlier shortcut:** if Mafuyu cannot name why she helped or lacks a stable `I like helping people` preference, the positive response to helping has little self-model value.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** Mafuyu explicitly recognizes `my small action helped Kanade -> Kanade is happy -> my chest feels warm`; card `0326` then shows remembered promise/follow-through toward Miku and satisfaction at keeping it.

**Guardrail:** contribution is one self-evidence route, not a moral obligation or generalized altruistic identity. Do not reproduce Kanade's pathological responsibility structure in Mafuyu by treating usefulness as the condition of worth.

### CR-N25-MF-065 - self-authored preservation gains tentative outward orientation

**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN through EVENT_0042**.

Mafuyu compares old OWN writing with current lyrics. She remembers having no desire to deliver the old material to anyone, whereas the recent lyric came from wanting to leave her own feeling in words and she now recognizes that being heard by someone is preferable to no one hearing it. Her final question—whether she is putting something into the lyrics—must remain tentative. EVENT_0042 establishes emergent communicative authorship, not a settled recipient-facing artistic mission.

### CR-N25-MF-063 - relational support can increase access without taking authorship

**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN through EVENT_0042**.

Ichika/Honami's reading of Mafuyu's lyrics, Miku's chronological song performance, and Miku/Luka's sensory scaffolding all alter access conditions. None is allowed to supply Mafuyu's final emotional label. External recognition can be evidence without becoming authority.

### CR-N25-MF-064 - affective access and categorical naming remain separable

**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN through EVENT_0042**.

Mafuyu notices, repeats, investigates, causally narrows, and positively appraises the warmth while still withholding a categorical name. The event therefore extends the sequence from autobiographical affect into relational affect without collapsing access into labeling.

### CR-N25-K-071 - ordinary social pleasure can re-enter Kanade's self-knowledge without resolving rescue/penance

**Earlier shortcut:** Kanade's meaningful positive states after the main story are primarily those that advance composition/rescue efficacy.  
**Transition:** **REJECT / broaden**.  
**Current authority:** Kanade experiences the mixed meeting as simply fun, reciprocates future-contact intent, retrieves a forgotten childhood friendship memory, and wonders whether parts of ordinary life have been lost while her life was narrowed by `music must save people`. Card `0327:02` preserves this recognition after the outing.

**Guardrail:** `K-E0026-01` remains current. Guilt, rescue mission, responsibility inflation, and poor self-care are not resolved.

### CR-N25-K-042 - positive destination becomes weakly reciprocal

**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN through EVENT_0042**.

Earlier releases established that Kanade can count partial positive downstream change in Mafuyu and broader listeners. EVENT_0042 adds a limited reciprocal direction: Mafuyu's small bridge helps produce an ordinary positive experience for Kanade, and Kanade explicitly recognizes the benefit. This does not equal symmetric rescue, but it shows that the relationship can generate positive destinations for Kanade as well as for Mafuyu.


## 7.15 EVENT_0044 claim transitions

### `CR-N25-FAMILY-060` - Shinonome sibling antagonism is compatible with reciprocal care, positive shared history, and causal influence

**Earlier current authority:** EVENT_0029 established that Ena/Akito's abrasive sibling grammar coexists with reciprocal practical care and that Ena's casual encouragement helped shape Akito's musical path.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN / broaden**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0044 adds a positive shared-childhood leisure substrate: Akito remembers camping with Ena and their mother as his most enjoyable camping experience. Core `0044:06` also shows sibling experience becoming practical risk knowledge: after hearing that Ena had serious trouble in the mountains, Akito proactively checks cellular/GPS conditions for his own group. Their care/influence should therefore not be modeled only as present-day errands, teasing, or emergency reciprocity; remembered shared life and retained causal knowledge also matter.

**Guardrail:** the evidence does not imply a consistently harmonious sibling relationship or establish the full details/source of Ena's mountain incident.

### `CR-N25-FAMILY-072` - Shinonome paternal role-mismatch is not confined to later artistic evaluation

**Earlier potential overread:** the father's painter/professional role conflicts with his paternal role only when judging Ena's artistic career or work.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN / REVISE scope**.  
**Current authority:** Akito's childhood camping account provides bounded independent evidence that the same role tension can appear in ordinary family life: the father continues painting while the family is camping, and Ena explicitly wants him to engage with them rather than remain absorbed in the painter role. This strengthens the EVENT_0014 interpretation that Ena's paternal injury involves role misattunement, not only the content of a later talent verdict.

**Guardrail:** a single remembered trip does not prove chronic neglect, malicious intent, or that this pattern alone caused Ena's later artistic-recognition vulnerability.

## 7.16 EVENT_0045 claim transitions

### `CR-N25-MZ-067` - attachment-preserving nondisclosure persists across enacted future time

**Earlier authority:** after EVENT_0039, nondisclosure can function partly as a strategy for preserving a valued relationship form.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0045 reaches the next New Year with the guarded issue still undisclosed. Mizuki is sincerely glad another year with the group has begun and wishes for this time to continue. This demonstrates that attachment-preserving deferral is not merely an immediate post-crisis rationalization; it remains behaviorally and emotionally compatible with genuine ongoing group enjoyment.

**Guardrail:** the silent wish does not prove Mizuki has consciously solved the tradeoff or that indefinite nondisclosure is costless.

### `CR-N25-K-071` - ordinary-life recovery coexists with persistent rescue obligation

**Earlier authority:** EVENT_0042 shows ordinary social pleasure and awareness of lost ordinary life can re-enter Kanade's self-knowledge without resolving rescue/penance.  
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN**.  
**Current authority:** EVENT_0045 supplies direct counterevidence to any reading that the EVENT_0042 insight displaced the salvation mission. Kanade participates in another ordinary outing, attends to practical comfort, and includes the Virtual Singers in ritual, yet tells Mafuyu she will keep trying and privately wishes to make music that saves everyone.

### `CR-N25-MF-073` - self-search can motivate chosen social participation without already yielding a stable self-definition

**Earlier shortcut:** continued inability to define the self should make Mafuyu largely passive about ordinary relational opportunities.  
**Transition:** **REJECT / broaden**.  
**Current authority:** Mafuyu identifies a possible evening window, volunteers that she will ask to join the group outing, actually attends, and privately keeps `find myself` as the goal. Chosen participation can therefore accompany unresolved self-search rather than requiring prior self-definition.

### `CR-N25-VS-074` - a Mafuyu-generated SEKAI can become socially populated without ceasing to be Mafuyu's SEKAI

**Earlier potential overread:** if the Empty SEKAI accumulates Virtual Singers, objects, and relationships, its original Mafuyu-specific psychological ontology is being replaced.  
**Transition:** **REJECT**.  
**Current authority:** N25 Miku herself holds both propositions in `shuffle_15_017`: the space is no longer literally a world with nobody because Mafuyu and the environment have changed, **and** it remains Mafuyu's feelings' SEKAI in which Miku remains `this SEKAI's Miku`. Social population is an extension of ontology, not cancellation of origin.


## 7.17 EVENT_0047 claim transitions

### `CR-N25-KM-075` - Kanade-Mafuyu reciprocity is constitutive at origin

**Earlier potential overread:** Kanade first acts as the active rescuer and Mafuyu as the passive recipient, with reciprocity emerging only later.
**Transition:** **REVISE / STRENGTHEN historical scope**.
**Current authority:** EVENT_0047 shows a reciprocal causal loop before direct collaboration stabilizes: K's music gives Mafuyu the first strongly differentiated affective stimulus and a route into self-directed creative inquiry; Snow's arrangement gives Kanade a unique depiction of despair that expands Kanade's composition and makes a small-light/reaching project possible. Present mutual retrospective valuation confirms that this origin remains meaningful.
**Guardrail:** reciprocity is not symmetry. Kanade's later rescue responsibility, dependency risk, and self-worth pathology remain more structurally centered on Mafuyu.

### `CR-N25-MF-076` - painful affect can initiate self-authored inquiry before categorical preference

**Earlier limitation:** the strongest explicit first-person-authority evidence for Mafuyu was concentrated in later negative-affect externalization and positive warmth retrieval.
**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN / extend backward**.
**Current authority:** before N25 forms, K's music uniquely produces painful non-null affect that Mafuyu cannot explain but cannot ignore. Mafuyu independently seeks a method to understand the difference and begins creative reconstruction. A positive preference label is not required for the response to become usable self-evidence.
**Guardrail:** this is an epistemic distinction, not a moral valuation of suffering.

### `CR-N25-VS-077` - SEKAI continuity can preserve once-born feeling traces without omniscient retrieval

**Earlier potential overread:** if a feeling becomes inaccessible or changes, the SEKAI either loses it entirely or can provide perfect direct retrieval because it originates in Mafuyu's feelings.
**Transition:** **REVISE both extremes**.
**Current authority:** N25 Miku explicitly tells Mafuyu that once-born feelings may be forgotten or change but do not simply cease to have existed, and that Miku/SEKAI remember Mafuyu's feelings. This supports continuity assurance while the existing non-omniscience rule remains intact.
**Guardrail:** do not convert manifestation memory into mind-reading, objective diagnosis, or an on-demand autobiographical database.

### Existing claims strengthened

- **`CR-N25-K-001` - STRENGTHEN:** EVENT_0047 directly shows that the post-father-collapse prosocial aspiration has already become compulsory rescue before Snow enters Kanade's life.
- **`CR-N25-K-002` - STRENGTHEN:** Kanade explicitly entangles saving others with moral legitimacy/right-to-live logic while continuing genuine concern for listeners.
- **`CR-N25-K-007` - STRENGTHEN:** linked area `_001` explicitly values accompaniment below a completed `saved` threshold.
- **`CR-N25-K-042` - STRENGTHEN:** the first K/Snow loop supplies historical evidence for recipient-responsive composition, while present Kanade can still value partial reach.
- **`CR-N25-K-071` - STRENGTHEN:** present ordinary gratitude, group pleasure, and chosen continuation coexist with one-meal self-care evidence and continuing rescue commitment.
- **`CR-N25-MF-009` and `CR-N25-MF-064` - STRENGTHEN:** affective access-before-labeling now has explicit pre-N25 provenance in K-song pain and later concern-label uncertainty.
- **`CR-N25-MZ-067` - STRENGTHEN:** card `0363` and area `_005` show Mizuki explicitly valuing the time Ena's waiting preserves while disclosure remains deferred.

## 7.18 EVENT_0051 claim transitions

EVENT_0051 requires no new governing claim ID. It materially strengthens the existing Mizuki/group model.

### `CR-N25-MZ-006` - STRENGTHEN

**Current authority extension:** school avoidance remains context-conditioned rather than generalized low social appetite. `monthly2202_001` shows that concern for a teacher's lost family time can immediately re-motivate a disliked make-up obligation without proving global attendance normalization.

### `CR-N25-MZ-008` - STRENGTHEN

Mizuki's design enthusiasm, teasing, ordinary invitations, live improvisation, child-facing mediation, and later admission of nerves jointly reinforce that the bright/playful register is a genuine baseline capacity, not merely a defensive performance.

### `CR-N25-MZ-009` - STRENGTHEN

Card `0387` makes the value of partial-disclosure trust unusually explicit: Mizuki privately feels gratitude that An continues to interact normally while An still lacks the guarded content. Real safety is therefore not contingent on total disclosure.

### `CR-N25-MZ-010` - STRENGTHEN

N25 companionship now supports individualized seasonal reciprocity. Mizuki's return gifts are recipient-specific and include the N25 Virtual Singers, extending ordinary relationship function beyond generic group co-presence.

### `CR-N25-MZ-034` - STRENGTHEN / REVISE upward

The subjective disclosure ceiling remains psychologically real, but EVENT_0051 adds more evidence that relationship depth, personalization, gratitude, and care can continue to increase before disclosure. The ceiling is not an objective cap on all intimacy.

### `CR-N25-MZ-035` - STRENGTHEN guardrail

EVENT_0051 provides strong non-crisis evidence that high-energy social behavior can be authentic enjoyment and competence. Combined with EVENT_0039, brightness must be treated as multi-determined rather than as either proof of wellness or proof of masking.

### `CR-N25-G-036` - STRENGTHEN

Ordinary companionship deepens through personalized gift ritual while the central epistemic asymmetry remains intact. Closeness and incomplete transparency continue to coexist.

### Bounded evidence not promoted into new claims

Kanade's tentative food-preference inference and Mafuyu-Luka page-turn accommodation are important D0 reconstruction evidence but remain too bounded to justify new governing claims at this release boundary.

```yaml
confidence: high | medium | low

```

Recognition is an input to self-assessment, not a sovereign permission slip.

**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN**.

### `CR-N25-ENA-022` — needing recognition is not evidence of fraudulent artistic motivation

**Transition:** **PRESERVE / STRENGTHEN**.

## 7.22 EVENT_0058 claim transitions

EVENT_0058 introduces no successor Mizuki state and requires no new governing claim ID. It materially strengthens the existing model.

### `CR-N25-MZ-006` — STRENGTHEN

School avoidance remains identity-threat- and context-conditioned. A conspicuous compulsory collective role is initially aversive, while meaningful creative agency, relational safety, and recipient-oriented purpose can produce sustained voluntary effort and genuine enjoyment without global attendance normalization.

### `CR-N25-MZ-008` — STRENGTHEN

Mizuki's energetic/creative register remains a genuine capacity. Card `0429` adds research-driven, symbolic, iterative craft behavior outside N25 rather than merely playful surface competence.

### `CR-N25-MZ-009` — STRENGTHEN

Partial-disclosure relationships can support substantial ordinary collaboration. Mizuki works closely with An/Rui and later the cheer group while guarded content remains undisclosed; relationship reality is not contingent on total transparency.

### `CR-N25-MZ-034` — STRENGTHEN / REVISE upward

EVENT_0058 sharpens the distinction between **subjective disclosure ceiling** and **objective relational ceiling**. Mizuki can even encourage Rui to expose a risky creative desire when local evidence supports safety while still experiencing their own guarded issue as unsafe. Disclosure judgment is content- and context-sensitive, not a single monotonic openness variable.

### `CR-N25-MZ-035` — STRENGTHEN guardrail

Mizuki's brightness during the cheer project is later first-person-confirmed as real enjoyment. Authentic positive affect can coexist with unresolved disclosure distress; neither “all masking” nor “therefore cured” is authorized.

Relational warmth now becomes context-level first-person knowledge (`ここはあったかい`) and is behaviorally consequential under competing obligation.

Contribution-linked warmth remains valid, while EVENT_0061 adds a complementary route based on **receiving** non-extractive companionship rather than causing another person's happiness.

### `CR-N25-FAMILY-079` — NEW / canonical bounded family formulation

**Claim:** genuine care and autonomy-eroding control can coexist in the same family relationship. Mafuyu's developmental problem requires differentiating forms of care rather than deciding that one side was never real.

### `CR-N25-G-080` — NEW / canonical

**Claim:** N25 functions in part as a context where Mafuyu need not perform the `good child`, allowing first-person signals to remain visible without immediate correction into externally approved behavior.

**Guardrail:** this does not authorize N25 to dictate anti-family choices or claim authority over Mafuyu's internal meaning.

### CR-N25-K-071 — ordinary social pleasure can re-enter Kanade's self-knowledge

**Transition:** **STRENGTHEN / CROSS_EVENT_VALIDATE.**

`I want others to smile -> others may want me to smile -> my enjoyment can matter to them -> occasional self-directed pleasure becomes morally permissible.`

## EVENT_0068 claim transitions

### `CR-N25-MZ-006` — STRENGTHEN

Historical withdrawal and present school evidence now converge on the same rule: Mizuki's school avoidance is identity-threat/context conditioned rather than evidence of low intelligence or generalized lack of social appetite. Academic comprehension can remain high while attendance, worksheets, and participation become selectively disengaged.

### `CR-N25-MZ-009` / `CR-N25-MZ-034` — STRENGTHEN

EVENT_0068 gives causal depth to partial-disclosure trust. Mizuki originally approaches K/Ena while fear remains, later acquires real ordinary-life attachment, and still does not disclose the guarded issue. The subjective disclosure ceiling is therefore not an objective ceiling on creative intimacy, companionship, or authentic positive affect.

### `CR-N25-MZ-067` — STRENGTHEN / causal backfill

Attachment-preserving nondisclosure now has a stronger historical explanation. Mizuki once chose a desired relationship despite rejection fear; because that approach succeeded, later disclosure threatens a relationship whose value has been behaviorally accumulated rather than imagined in the abstract.

Card `0490` further strengthens the guardrail that waiting is not automatically stagnation. Luka explicitly revises her own intervention logic when she recognizes that forced questioning may damage a relationship that is otherwise still moving.

### `CR-N25-MZ-083` — NEW / CANONICAL

**Claim:** Mizuki can make authentic approach choices toward desired belonging while rejection fear remains active; courage/approach behavior is not equivalent to disclosure readiness.

**Evidence:** the first K/Ena collaboration, desire to accept Kanade's invitation, fear that wanted people may leave if they see Mizuki as strange, sister's fear-preserving encouragement, Mizuki's `まだ怖い……怖いけど` decision to try, and present retrospective recognition that the resulting N25 life is genuinely enjoyable.

**Guardrail:** do not use earlier approach courage as proof that present guarded disclosure should now be easy or morally required.

### `CR-N25-MZ-084` — NEW / CANONICAL bounded speech rule

**Claim:** a fast, smooth explanation from Mizuki can function as rehearsed boundary maintenance rather than evidence that the subject is emotionally uncomplicated.

**Evidence:** card `0490` Luka notices that Mizuki's answer about school emerges with unusual fluency and considers that repeated questioning may have produced a canned safe explanation.

**Guardrail:** fluency alone never proves concealment. Use this rule only when topic history, repeated omission, and other evidence support the boundary-maintenance interpretation.

### `CR-N25-G-036` — STRENGTHEN

Ordinary companionship and significant incomplete transparency coexist across a much longer causal arc than previously demonstrated: the group originates in useful creative difference, becomes wanted, accumulates photos/outings/karaoke/café work, and remains attachment-bearing without total disclosure.

## EVENT_0070 claim transitions

## EVENT_0076 claim review — I1 corroboration without a new governing formulation

The disclosure does not reveal the collapse history to Ichika or imply acquittal in Kanade's self-understanding.

Ordinary receptive pleasure persists within `K-E0063-01`; ongoing household dependence and restricted food purchasing remain visible.

Preserve `MF-E0072-01 / K-E0063-01 / E-E0070-01 / MZ-E0039-01`. ## EVENT_0077 claim transitions — informed support, contextual wanting and usable permission

### `CR-N25-E-091` — NEW / CANONICAL

**Claim:** Ena can revise agency advice when direct disclosure shows that she projected her own available assertiveness onto Mafuyu. Effective support then includes differentiating histories, asking the person's want and making bounded room to enact it.

**Evidence:** Mafuyu corrects the “never tried” premise; Ena considers the counterfactual of a different upbringing, then offers help as a peer (`PJSK:event:0077:06:002:0020–0084`). Her portrait is expressly a fallible perception (`PJSK:event:0077:07:002:0159–0173`). **Limit:** no universal empathy/mediation competence. The earlier phone takeover, instruction to stay quiet and invented pretext remain part of the same event, not exemplary negotiated care.

### `CR-N25-MF-092` — NEW / CANONICAL

**Claim:** Mafuyu's explicitly wanted collaborative music activity can coexist with maternal deference, concealed participation and uncertainty about the activity's larger emotional or identity meaning.

**Evidence:** wanting songs with everyone while unable to explain acting against maternal instructions or affirm Ena's “important” category (`PJSK:event:0077:06:002:0040–0071`; `PJSK:event:0077:07:002:0125–0129`); later spontaneous work and valued conversation (`PJSK:card:0542:02:002`). **Limit:** not a career choice, settled autonomous principle, global affect recovery or complete home independence.

### `CR-N25-FAMILY-093` — NEW / CANONICAL, context-bounded

**Claim:** apparently similar parental permission language must be interpreted through its consequences and the child's experienced room to differ. Warm wording or absence of argument cannot alone establish usable choice.

**Evidence:** the two mothers' personal-choice formulations produce different felt contexts for Mafuyu; regret/future reasoning accompanies surrendering music, whereas ordinary Shinonome disagreement coexists with her chest lightening (`PJSK:event:0077:04:002:0052–0106`; `PJSK:event:0077:06:002:0027–0040`). **Limit:** no denial of genuine attachment/care, idealization of the whole Shinonome family, or omniscient claim about maternal computer-search intent. Ena's recalled freedom to draw is briefly considered and defensively dismissed, not reconciliation (`PJSK:card:0540:02:001:0042–0056`).

### `CR-N25-MF-088` — REFINE chronological scope; retain bounded-wanting rule

The valid EVENT_0072 rule is that action-specific wanting and continuation preference can be accessible before broad emotion/preference/life-direction fluency. EVENT_0077's explicit report of an earlier attempt to tell her mother she wanted music **rules out extending EVENT_0072's “first explicit” language into a first-ever desire statement** (`PJSK:event:0077:06:002:0022–0040`). Earlier wording remains a historical analytical handoff, qualified by this revision. Do not reconstruct a universal pre-EVENT_0072 inability to voice any want or project Ena's newly corrected knowledge backward. Preserve `MF-E0072-01` with context-dependent articulation/enactment, not a monotonic linguistic recovery model.
